<b>WAN Transformation </b>challenge?</p>
</h2><div class="item-wrapper"><div class="item"><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/MPLS-migration-to-SD-WAN-d0e6b3dcc0e1791fd049058d6fb87870.svg"/><a class="cato-button cato-button oval green-outline mid" href="/solutions/#mpls-migration-to-sdwan">MPLS Migration to SD-WAN</a></div><div class="item"><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/Optimize-Global-Connectivity-cb9f9ebe32164f3a12eec34defac1060.svg"/><a class="cato-button cato-button oval green-outline mid" href="/solutions/#optimized-global-connectivity">Optimized Global Connectivity</a></div><div class="item"><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/Secure-Branch-Internet-Access-e1b095cbee8456544d641f1379ebb7ff.svg"/><a class="cato-button cato-button oval green-outline mid" href="/solutions/#secure-branch-internet-access">Secure Branch Internet Access</a></div><div class="item"><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/Cloud-Acceleration-and-Control-c23edccf4e80659a5d3dda36902bc107.svg"/><a class="cato-button cato-button oval green-outline mid" href="/solutions/#optimized-and-secure-cloud-access">Cloud Acceleration and Control</a></div><div class="item"><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/Mobile-Security-and-Optimization-60a63ff7de1ccffead6e6bc6ff08b1e2.svg"/><a class="cato-button cato-button oval green-outline mid" href="/solutions/#optimized-and-secure-mobile-access">Mobile Security and Optimization</a></div><div class="item"><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/Flexible-Management-Service-1727e458edacbd6922a1cce59ee46647.svg"/><a class="cato-button cato-button oval green-outline mid" href="/services/">Flexible Network Management </a></div></div></div></div><section class="versus-section"><h2><p>Complex or <strong>Simple</strong>?</p>
</h2><h3><p>Network complexity is overloading IT and slowing down the business. It is time to make the network <strong>simple</strong>.</p>
</h3><div class="vs-wrapper"><div class="item left"><h3>Point Solutions</h3><h4>Complex</h4><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/compare_rigid-thing-777cdf0ffbb386868c4b5f6be821b69f.svg"/><div class="text"><p>Managing complex networks built with multiple third-party products is difficult. And, outsourcing complexity to a telco results in a slow and rigid service.  Why? because the telcos, like your IT team, have to manage and support products they don’t own or control.</p>
</div></div><div class="vs">VS.</div><div class="item right"><h3>Cato Cloud</h3><h4>Simple</h4><img alt="" class="cato-image" id="" src="/static/compare_ring-6ae9ac85e4c8f5b9f8e1db9da2a1c592.svg"/><div class="text"><p>Cato built a cloud-native network from the ground up, as a single, converged cloud service. Because we built it, own it, and run it – service is affordable, changes are quick, expert support is always there, and innovation happens at cloud-speed.</p>
</div></div></div></section><section class="one-platform off"><h2><p><strong>One platform.</strong> Gradual deployment.</p>
